## 1. Purpose

This is the canonical mutable ledger for **who knows what, when, from which source, with what confidence, and whether the knowledge is direct, inferred, mistaken, or audience-only**.

A psychologically accurate character with the wrong information is still an inaccurate reconstruction. Therefore every scenario or longitudinal interpretation should resolve:

> **temporal state + relationship state + knowledge state**

before generating behavior or dialogue.

Current populated N25 entries integrate epistemic evidence through EVENT_0053. EVENT_0030, EVENT_0031, EVENT_0037, EVENT_0040, EVENT_0043, and EVENT_0046 were fully screened at I0; EVENT_0038 adds audience-richer Rui context without new participant transmission; EVENT_0039 expands direct knowledge about the *existence and support boundaries* of Mizuki's guarded conflict while preserving the guarded content itself as undisclosed; EVENT_0041 adds bounded ordinary Mizuki-An mutual knowledge; EVENT_0042 gives Mafuyu new first-person knowledge about relational warmth/contribution, gives Kanade bounded self-knowledge about forgotten ordinary life, and expands cross-unit public creative knowledge without transmitting N25-private biography; EVENT_0044 adds narrowly bounded Shinonome-family knowledge on Akito's side and a childhood family anecdote shared with VBS peers; EVENT_0045 adds second-year ritual/VS continuity knowledge; EVENT_0047 adds bounded N25 origin-history transmission, Kanade/Mafuyu first-person historical understanding, and N25 Miku/SEKAI continuity evidence while preserving the full flashback interiority as audience-only unless explicitly transmitted; EVENT_0051 adds Mizuki safe-normality self-knowledge, personalized-care transmission, bounded Kanade preference inference, and Mafuyu-Luka accommodation without guarded-content leakage; EVENT_0052 adds bounded Mizuki-Shizuku/MMJ craft-collaboration knowledge. EVENT_0053 adds Ena/N25 knowledge of her art-school history and present training choice, while preserving audience-only detail around earlier paternal injury; it also makes reciprocal N25 creative influence explicit. Material later than EVENT_0053 remains pending.

## 2. Knowledge classes

- **DIRECT_WITNESS** — character directly observed event/statement.
- **DIRECT_DISCLOSURE** — another participant explicitly told them.
- **INFERENCE_STRONG** — well-supported inference from observed evidence.
- **INFERENCE_WEAK** — plausible but not established.
- **BELIEF_INCORRECT_OR_DISTORTED** — sincerely held but source-corrected belief.
- **AUDIENCE_ONLY / AUDIENCE_RICHER** — analytical knowledge not available to participant.
- **UNKNOWN_TRANSMISSION** — source does not establish whether information was later passed on.
- **OPEN** — unresolved.

## 3. N25 audience knowledge at main-story endpoint

### EPI-N25-AUD-01 — audience-richer causal knowledge

**Status:** canonical analytical knowledge; prohibited as automatic character premise.

The audience knows in greater detail:

- exact progression from Mafuyu's nursing preference to externally reinforced doctor identity;
- interrupted arcade preference;
- progressive loss of taste, enjoyment, and confidence in internal sensation;
- distributed social system reinforcing the good-girl identity;
- full diary-based context around Kanade's father's professional crisis;
- the extent to which Kanade's guilt simplifies a multicausal collapse.

**Rule:** These facts may guide analysis but cannot automatically appear in character dialogue or reasoning.

## 4. Mafuyu endpoint knowledge — EPI-N25-MF-01

**Temporal boundary:** `nightcode_01_20` endpoint.

### Known by direct disclosure / direct interaction

Mafuyu knows:

- Kanade's father was a composer;
- Kanade believes her music contributed to his collapse;
- Kanade has wanted to disappear;
- composition functions as part of Kanade's reason to continue;
- Kanade's desire to save Mafuyu is partly entangled with Kanade's own need for continuation;
- Ena values OWN's work and does not want to lose it;
- Mizuki sees structural similarity between their pressures and would feel lonely if Mafuyu disappeared;
- Nightcord members have first-person reasons for wanting continued relationship;
- Kanade perceives a recurring “Mafuyu sound” in Mafuyu's music;
- Mafuyu herself has recognized wanting to be found and has agreed to continue searching for now.

### Belief / interpretation

- Kanade is “cursed” by her father's words and rescue obligation — source supports this as Mafuyu's interpretation and as a serious analytical description, but it is not a complete objective causal model.

### Not known / prohibited assumptions

- audience's full multicausal correction to Kanade's guilt;
- any later-event developments;
- complete private histories of Ena or Mizuki beyond disclosed/observed material.

## 5. Kanade endpoint knowledge — EPI-N25-KF-01

Kanade knows:

- Mafuyu is Snow and OWN;
- Mafuyu has been searching for real feelings/self;
- failure of that search can intensify disappearance desire;
- Mafuyu can experience hope itself as exhausting after repeated failure;
- an old Kanade song reached Mafuyu sufficiently to exist inside the Empty SEKAI;
- Mafuyu ultimately recognizes wanting to be found;
- Mafuyu has accepted continued search for now;
- Ena and Mizuki have distinct reasons for remaining involved rather than a shared rescue ideology.

### Not known / prohibited assumptions

Kanade does **not** know the complete childhood/middle-school developmental sequence through which Mafuyu's preferences were displaced. She should not reason as though she personally witnessed:

- the original nursing aspiration;
- the doctor redirection in full causal detail;
- the interrupted arcade preference;
- the complete taste/affect flattening sequence;
- every family/teacher/peer reinforcement mechanism.

## 6. Mizuki endpoint knowledge — EPI-N25-MZF-01

Mizuki knows:

- Mafuyu's mother has exerted normative pressure, based partly on an overheard interaction;
- Snow = OWN = Mafuyu;
- the Empty-SEKAI rupture and Mafuyu's serious disappearance risk;
- their own analogous experience of conformity pressure;
- Kanade's disclosed father/guilt history from the confrontation;
- Mafuyu's self-search and the post-crisis decision to continue searching.

### Knowledge character

Mizuki's understanding is **directionally strong but incomplete**. Much of it is `INFERENCE_STRONG`, not omniscient biography.

### Prohibited assumption

Do not allow Mizuki to speak as though they know every mechanism of Mafuyu's childhood identity erosion. Their main-story intervention style explicitly preserves Mafuyu's epistemic ownership.

## 7. Ena endpoint knowledge — EPI-N25-EF-01

Ena knows:

- Snow = OWN = Mafuyu;
- Mafuyu's disappearance risk is serious;
- Mafuyu's problem concerns self-discovery rather than artistic acclaim;
- Mafuyu has accepted continued searching;
- Nightcord has reconstituted after the crisis;
- Mizuki observed family-related pressure and sees structural similarity;
- Mafuyu's work matters intensely to Ena even when Ena remains angry with Mafuyu.

### Unknown transmission

`OPEN / UNKNOWN_TRANSMISSION:` whether Ena receives the entirety of Kanade's episode-17 father/guilt disclosure. The source does not justify granting Ena detailed knowledge merely because the audience heard it or because other N25 members did.

### Prohibited assumption

Do not use Kanade's full father history as an Ena dialogue premise until a later source establishes transmission.

## 8. Group knowledge-state progression

### EPI-N25-G-0 — anonymous collaboration

**Boundary:** P01.

- members know work habits and aesthetic sensibilities;
- legal names/faces/offline lives largely unknown;
- Snow/OWN identity hidden;
- Empty SEKAI knowledge extremely limited and uneven.

### EPI-N25-G-1 — identity exposure

**Boundary:** P02.

- Snow = Mafuyu = OWN becomes group-relevant knowledge;
- Empty SEKAI crisis is shared evidence;
- creative familiarity is revealed to be much greater than biographical knowledge.

### EPI-N25-G-2 — differentiated private knowledge

**Boundary:** P03-P04.

- audience receives much richer individual backstory than the group;
- Ena confirms private disappearance vulnerability outside shared group knowledge;
- Mizuki develops strong structural inference from personal analogy;
- Kanade confronts own guilt and rescue logic;
- full autobiographical knowledge remains partitioned.

### EPI-N25-G-3 — confrontation disclosure / post-main partial knowledge

**Boundary:** P05 endpoint.

- Mafuyu receives major Kanade disclosure;
- Mizuki receives Kanade disclosure and already has family-pressure inference;
- Ena's receipt of full Kanade disclosure is uncertain;
- all four know real identities and basic crisis stakes;
- no one becomes omniscient.

**State principle:** N25 is more embodied and more informed, not fully transparent.

## 8.1 EVENT_0002 knowledge-state integration

### EPI-N25-MF-E0002 — Mafuyu after 囚われのマリオネット

Mafuyu now has direct/experiential evidence that:

- unwanted affect can still be hers;
- a feeling need not be understood before it can be expressed;
- creative work can externalize and return otherwise inaccessible affect;
- another person's prompt does not automatically make the resulting feeling externally authored;
- relational presence can modulate distress even when she cannot name the resulting state;
- N25 can receive blunt/unpolished reactions without immediately abandoning her.

She still does **not** possess a complete causal theory of her identity erosion or stable ordinary preference map.

### EPI-N25-K-E0002 — Kanade after 囚われのマリオネット

Kanade now knows that:

- Mafuyu's flatness contains discriminable reactions;
- questioning can reveal clues but can also overwhelm Mafuyu;
- N25 Miku cannot simply report Mafuyu's current feelings;
- Mafuyu herself may need to notice/express what others cannot find for her;
- lyrics can serve as an access path unavailable in direct explanation.

Kanade's inference that Mafuyu “only thinks she cannot feel” is **partial**, not omniscient truth.

### EPI-N25-E-E0002 — Ena after 囚われのマリオネット

Ena now knows that Mafuyu's direct reactions can be creatively useful, that intervention can carry destabilization risk, and that Mafuyu's affect problem is not equivalent to total absence of response. Detailed Kanade-father knowledge remains subject to the prior `UNKNOWN_TRANSMISSION` constraint.

### EPI-N25-MZ-E0002 — Mizuki after 囚われのマリオネット

Mizuki strengthens the marionette/external-control inference, observes improved group atmosphere after the outing, and knows that Mafuyu still does not naturally understand why the others care beyond production roles. Structural insight remains `INFERENCE_STRONG`, not complete biography.

### EPI-N25-MIKU-E0002 — N25 Miku manifestation constraint

**Status:** `ACTIVE_PROVISIONAL`; manifestation-specific.

- privileged familiarity with Empty SEKAI persistent structures;
- can observe changes and infer psychological relevance;
- cannot directly retrieve Mafuyu's complete current mental state;
- marks conjecture as conjecture;
- can mirror ownership (“that feeling is Mafuyu's”) without authoring a final meaning for Mafuyu.

**Reconstruction prohibition:** do not use N25 Miku or the SEKAI as a transparent mind-reading device.

### EPI-N25-G-4 — group knowledge after EVENT_0002

- group knows the marionette triggered strong Mafuyu affect;
- Mizuki shares the strong control/mother analogy with the group;
- group learns that candid reaction and creative externalization can reveal otherwise inaccessible affect;
- knowledge remains partial: no member acquires the audience's complete Mafuyu developmental biography.

## 8.2 EVENT_0004 knowledge-state integration

### EPI-N25-MF-E0004 — Mafuyu's Emu-salience inference

Mafuyu directly knows that Emu reacts to her unlike most school juniors. By card `0127`, she can formulate a tentative hypothesis that Emu may not perceive her as the expected “good girl.” She also notices Emu as novel/fresh and returns to the topic without knowing why it matters.

**Truth status:** supported observation + partial self-inference.  
**Constraint:** do not upgrade this to conscious liking, intimacy, or full awareness of why Emu is salient.

### EPI-CROSS-EMU-MF-E0004 — Emu's mask-incongruence observation

Emu directly observes that some of Mafuyu's smiles feel unlike spontaneous smiles and that post-race / low-stakes expressions can sometimes feel more genuine.

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_WITNESS + AFFECTIVE_INFERENCE_STRONG`.  
**Truth status:** partial.  
**Constraint:** Emu does not know Nightcord's crisis history, Empty SEKAI, family-developmental mechanisms, or the authoritative content of Mafuyu's internal state.

### EPI-N25-G-E0004 — Nightcord receives latent-salience evidence

Through Mafuyu's own account, Nightcord learns that an unusual school junior produced a response Mafuyu found novel. Kanade notices that Mafuyu volunteered this topic rather than merely answering a question and suggests that significance may exist before Mafuyu understands it.

**Constraint:** the group does not know Emu's private “real smile” judgments unless a later source explicitly transmits them.

## 8.3 EVENT_0007 knowledge-state integration

### EPI-N25-MZ-E0007 — Mizuki after KAMIKOU FESTIVAL！

Mizuki directly learns/recognizes that:

- An is deliberately attentive to scrutiny risk and can make participation safer without demanding disclosure;
- Rui has found companions and is visibly happier than during their shared rooftop-isolation period;
- Toya's life changed after Tsukasa explicitly authorized first-person choice and refusal of an imposed path;
- Akito is Ena's brother and Toya's trusted partner;
- N25 occupies enough relational space that Mizuki repeatedly imagines them in ordinary festival life;
- collective belonging can feel desirable without requiring aesthetic assimilation, as enacted by the customized class T-shirt.

Mizuki still does **not** know whether school will become consistently safe, whether future N25 festival plans will occur, or whether greater social attachment will make deeper disclosure safer.

### EPI-CROSS-RUI-MZ-E0007 — reciprocal companion inference

Rui directly observes/strongly infers that Mizuki now has people who function approximately as new companions. Mizuki directly observes Rui's new companion network and softened/happier social presentation.

**Constraint:** neither inference grants either character complete knowledge of the other's unit history or private crises.

### EPI-CROSS-AN-MZ-E0007 — partial-disclosure friendship knowledge

An learns that Mizuki has an online creative circle including Ena and has a long-standing history with Rui. Mizuki explicitly knows that An does **not** have complete disclosure of Mizuki's private issue.

**Truth status:** mutually supported bounded knowledge.  
**Constraint:** An's tact and safety do not imply that she knows what has not been told to her.

### EPI-CROSS-AKITO-TOYA-MZ-E0007 — school acquaintance boundary

Akito and Toya learn that Mizuki is Ena's friend/creative associate and establish direct school acquaintance. They do not acquire Nightcord's crisis history, Mafuyu's condition, Kanade's guilt history, or Mizuki's undisclosed private issue.

### EPI-N25-G-E0007 — Nightcord receives Mizuki's festival projection

Through card `0139:02`, Kanade, Ena, and Mafuyu learn that:

- Mizuki enjoyed the festival strongly enough to remain visibly energized afterward;
- Mizuki met Ena's brother;
- Mizuki spent the event imagining how the other N25 members would react to ordinary attractions;
- Mizuki wants future offline festival participation with the group.

The group does **not** automatically learn the full Rui–Mizuki rooftop history or all school-stigma experiences; later disclosure must establish those transmissions.

## 8.4 EVENT_0009 knowledge-state integration

### EPI-N25-G-E0009 — ordinary-social availability becomes shared group knowledge

Through the countdown itself, all four humans directly learn that:

- N25 can gather for a purely social reason without a work or crisis trigger;
- refusal of one activity format does not equal rejection of the relationship;
- the Empty SEKAI can be used for leisure and seasonal ritual;
- Miku can participate in such ritual and receive ordinary comfort/care from them;
- each member is willing to contribute in a way compatible with personal constraints.

**Constraint:** this knowledge does not imply equal enthusiasm for all future outings or shared access to one another's private histories.

### EPI-N25-MZ-E0009 — adaptive social planning confirmed

Mizuki now has direct evidence that their wish for ordinary N25 companionship is behaviorally realizable. Ena/Kanade's outdoor refusal can be answered by changing practical conditions rather than interpersonal pressure; Mafuyu can join around family/academic constraints.

### EPI-N25-ENA-E0009 — reciprocal ordinary relationship evidence

Ena directly experiences N25's social gathering as worthwhile enough to prepare for and later describes the difficult year as not entirely bad. She also now knows Mizuki as a reliable first-sale shopping/coordination partner.

### EPI-N25-MIKU-E0009 — shared-space and warmth knowledge

N25 Miku directly experiences:

- the humans arriving simply to play;
- collective celebration in the Empty SEKAI;
- material comfort objects from all four members;
- warmth as a positive sensory/relational category.

**Constraint:** this remains manifestation-specific evidence and does not revise the existing prohibition against treating N25 Miku as a transparent mind-reading device.

## 8.5 EVENT_0014 knowledge-state integration

### EPI-N25-G-E0014 - Ena recognition/talent wound becomes shared group knowledge

Through Ena's direct disclosure, Kanade, Mafuyu, and Mizuki now know that:

- Ena entered a serious art contest and received no recognition;
- Nightcord comments had been functioning as evidence that her artwork mattered;
- she encountered a viewer distinction between artwork that functions with a Nightcord song and artwork valued independently;
- a parent previously told her she lacked artistic talent;
- she partly believes that judgment;
- fear of never being recognized can make continued drawing feel impossible;
- she hates the part of herself that seems to need recognition in order to continue.

**Constraint:** the group does not automatically know the full middle-school school-choice scene, the exact history of Ena's painter aspiration, or the father's private rationale to Akito.

### EPI-AUDIENCE-ENA-FAMILY-E0014 - privileged causal history

The audience additionally knows:

- young Ena expected to pursue an art-track school, become a painter, and eventually surpass her father;
- her father globally rejected that future on talent/professional-survival grounds;
- Ena could not attend the art-focused school she wanted;
- the father privately tells Akito that his harshness comes from knowledge of artistic isolation and lifelong suffering;
- Akito tells him the missing act is parental rather than professional: look at Ena's art as her father.

**Constraint:** do not leak this complete causal model into Nightcord dialogue without later transmission.

### EPI-N25-ENA-E0014 - relational artistic value becomes differentiated from universal verdict

Ena directly learns that:

- Kanade originally found her work compelling and did not recruit her as interchangeable labor;
- Kanade can identify concrete ways Ena's prior illustrations changed how Nightcord songs communicated;
- Mizuki likes Ena's art regardless of the father's or public verdict;
- Mafuyu does not consider recognition desire shameful;
- Mafuyu can genuinely evaluate an Ena picture positively;
- the group will mobilize creatively when Ena, rather than Mafuyu, is endangered.

**Truth status:** supported relational knowledge.  
**Constraint:** none of these propositions proves that Ena has the professional talent required for the career she wants.

### EPI-N25-MF-E0014 - recognition motive and none-versus-unknown distinction

Mafuyu directly learns that:

- another creator can be deeply motivated by recognition without that motive being reducible to simple vanity;
- Ena can become angry at criticism and still want the criticism because improvement matters more than immediate comfort;
- Ena's persistence is evidence of genuine attachment to drawing;
- Ena distinguishes "there is nothing I like" from "I do not know what I like";
- Ena considers Mafuyu's impaired taste worth investigating through a practical medical-information route.

Mafuyu considers these distinctions but does not thereby gain stable ordinary preference access or a medical diagnosis.

### EPI-N25-K-E0014 - support under incomplete causal knowledge

Kanade directly recognizes that she does not know why Ena collapsed. She nevertheless knows enough to communicate:

- Ena is not alone;
- Ena's art is necessary to Nightcord's finished work;
- specific earlier illustrations had concrete compositional value.

**Behavioral consequence:** Kanade can act under uncertainty without pretending the uncertainty is gone, then trust/wait after the bounded message is delivered.

### EPI-N25-MZ-E0014 - limits of mood reading and post-intervention trust

Mizuki learns that ordinary shopping/food contact can improve Ena's visible mood without revealing the underlying cause. Their initial inference that Ena may soon be fine is incomplete. After the direct intervention, Mizuki learns that concern does not always justify continued action; once Ena reclaims agency, trusting and waiting can be the more autonomy-respecting response.

### EPI-N25-RIN-E0014 - manifestation-specific shared-SEKAI inference

N25 Rin and N25 Miku explicitly treat Rin's appearance as potentially connected not only to Mafuyu but also to the wishes of Kanade, Ena, and Mizuki after Mafuyu accepted them into the SEKAI.

**Truth status:** strong manifestation-specific inference, not universal cosmological proof.  
**Constraint:** do not generalize to other Virtual Singer manifestations or treat Rin as omniscient.

### EPI-FAMILY-ENA-E0014 - asymmetrical father-intent knowledge

Akito knows the father's stated rationale and communicates a simplified version to Ena: the father is worried and does not act from simple malice. Ena receives that information but does not possess the whole audience-known conversation and explicitly does not treat good intent as sufficient repair.

Ena does directly receive one new proposition from the father: he can inspect a specific work, identify what he thinks succeeds, and then offer technical criticism. This modifies what Ena knows about his artistic gaze without resolving what she believes about his paternal acceptance.

## 8.6 EVENT_0019 knowledge-state integration

### EPI-N25-MZ-E0019 - future attachment and disclosure conflict become direct self-knowledge

Mizuki directly knows that:

- N25 now matters enough that Mizuki wants to remain with the group into the future;
- this future-continuity desire is historically unusual for Mizuki;
- the group's ordinary outings can be genuinely enjoyable and can reveal meaningful new sides of other members;
- a guarded self-bound issue remains something Mizuki believes cannot be told to the group;
- the expectation of failure comes from repeated prior experience Mizuki understands as disclosure being futile;
- Mizuki personally fears that nondisclosure prevents the relationship from becoming the "true friendship" now desired.

**Constraint:** the source does not yet disclose the later-explicated full content of the guarded issue. Do not backfill it from future material.

### EPI-N25-ENA-E0019 - detected distress without causal access

Ena directly observes an unusual Mizuki expression on the return trip and later confirms that ordinary verbal reassurance does not completely settle her concern. She checks whether Akito has noticed anything at school and later invites Mizuki shopping.

Ena knows **that something may be wrong**, not **what it is**. Mizuki's ordinary fashion/food enthusiasm does not provide Ena with a causal explanation.

### EPI-CROSS-RUI-MZ-E0019 - suspected distress under explicit deflection

Rui observes Mizuki returning toward the rooftop and asks whether something was wrong. Mizuki denies having a reason to seek Rui. Rui therefore has evidence of possible concealed distress but no direct disclosure of its content.

**Behavioral consequence:** Rui leaves the rooftop available without forcing a confession.

### EPI-CROSS-RUI-MZ-E0038 - audience-richer Rui history / no new transmission

**Knowledge class:** `AUDIENCE_RICHER`.

EVENT_0038 gives the audience substantially richer causal knowledge of Rui's side of the Mizuki-Rui outsider relationship:

- young Rui experiences explicit difference-labeling and repeated failure to communicate why his interests are enjoyable;
- his childhood isolation is not reducible to peer rejection: his own difficulty accommodating others' fear and approaching across the gap also contributes;
- present Rui explicitly values trust as necessary for ambitious collaborative action and defines his show-making dream around crossing barriers that he could not cross as a child.

**Mizuki knowledge:** no new direct knowledge is established. EVENT_0038 contains no Mizuki appearance or disclosure to Mizuki, and the source does not establish that Rui later transmits the detailed childhood events.

**Rui knowledge of Mizuki:** unchanged. EVENT_0038 gives Rui no new information about Mizuki's guarded issue, N25 history, or EVENT_0019 disclosure conflict.

**Reconstruction guardrail:** analysts may use EVENT_0038 to understand why Rui's EVENT_0019 non-forcing availability is psychologically coherent, but must not let Mizuki quote or assume Rui's audience-only childhood details; must not claim Rui consciously maps his childhood onto Mizuki unless a later source says so; and must not collapse Rui's and Mizuki's outsider histories into the same mechanism.

### EPI-N25-MEIKO-E0019 - observer hypothesis with explicit uncertainty

N25 MEIKO knows/strongly infers that:

- Mafuyu's relationship with Kanade, Ena, and Mizuki is deeply consequential to the Empty SEKAI;
- interaction with those three is associated with ongoing SEKAI change and new manifestation history;
- the other three also carry unresolved material;
- Mizuki's concealed burden is especially salient;
- Mizuki cares strongly about the group.

MEIKO explicitly does **not** know why she manifested, what Mizuki's burden is, or what final action is appropriate. Her model remains observational and provisional.

### EPI-N25-RIN-E0019 - correction to the Mizuki/Mafuyu equivalence

When MEIKO wonders whether Mizuki is searching for a true self in a Mafuyu-like way, Rin supplies direct counterevidence: Mizuki visibly possesses and expresses preferences, especially around clothes, ribbons, and `カワイイ` aesthetics.

**Current analytical truth status:** structural similarity around norm pressure is supported; shared missing-self mechanism is not.

### EPI-N25-MF-E0019 - tentative positive affect and future-repeat inference

Mafuyu directly experiences the trip as possibly interesting, independently searches the shared photos for the cherry tree, accepts Mizuki's idea that the in-person experience may have contained a special feeling, and privately thinks a trip like this might be worth doing again someday.

**Constraint:** most of the group does not receive Mafuyu's final private future-repeat conclusion.

### EPI-N25-K-E0019 - creative blockage and help-seeking knowledge

Kanade directly learns that solitary effort may fail to restore composition, that asking the group and trying unfamiliar experience can generate new material, and that stories about unknown suffering can re-engage the salvation mission.

This is coping/method knowledge, not a replacement of Kanade's core rescue belief.

### EPI-N25-G-E0019 - mutual ordinary knowledge expands while core asymmetry persists

The group learns new ordinary information through the trip and later outings: fear responses, stamina, siblings, aesthetic habits, school-like behavior, and ordinary preferences. The group does **not** learn Mizuki's disclosure conflict or guarded issue.

**Authority statement:** EVENT_0019 demonstrates that increasing mutual knowledge and retaining major epistemic asymmetry can happen simultaneously.


## 8.7 EVENT_0026 knowledge-state integration

### EPI-N25-K-E0026 - destination, positive inheritance, and partial efficacy become direct knowledge

Kanade directly learns that:

- repeated emotionally intense songs can fail when she has not imagined where she wants the listener to arrive;
- her father once composed primarily to make his wife and daughter smile, and the warmth/gentleness of that music remains available as positive inheritance;
- she had partly sealed away happy family memory because remembering the happiness was itself painful;
- for Mafuyu, Kanade's desired destination includes self-discovery, broader feeling capacity, warmth, and genuine smile;
- Mafuyu's small spontaneous smile/warmth can be genuine progress even when Kanade still judges complete salvation unfinished;
- Mizuki's accompaniment can materially change how Kanade receives memory and solves a creative/psychological problem.

**Distorted belief preserved:** Kanade still believes she is decisively responsible for the father's collapse and explicitly denies herself a right to grieve on that basis.

### EPI-N25-MZ-E0026 - Kanade's penance structure becomes directly available to Mizuki

Mizuki directly receives or strongly infers that:

- Kanade's father can currently occupy a memory state from before Kanade's birth and fail to recognize Kanade;
- Kanade interprets the father's pre-Kanade happiness as evidence against herself;
- Kanade ties successful Mafuyu rescue to whether she has a right to remain;
- composition functions partly as self-punishment/repayment;
- simple reassurance that Kanade is blameless is unlikely to change the governing belief;
- companionship, memory search, and personally grounded evidence of Kanade's positive impact can still help.

**Constraint:** Mizuki does not thereby know the complete objective multicausal history of the father's collapse beyond what the integrated sources establish as transmitted.

### EPI-N25-MF-E0026 - spontaneous warmth, change hypothesis, and comparative sensory valence

Mafuyu directly experiences/learns that:

- Kanade's new song produces a smile before Mafuyu consciously notices the smile;
- Mafuyu can identify a small feeling of warmth even without a full explanation;
- the populated Empty SEKAI and the presence of Kanade/Ena/Mizuki/Virtual Singers are not inherently painful to her;
- Miku interprets ongoing SEKAI change as connected to Mafuyu no longer being alone;
- “I may be changing” is plausible enough to motivate a voluntary sensory experiment;
- taste remains poorly identified, but one food can register as less unpleasant than ordinary eating.

**Constraint:** Mafuyu does not receive Kanade's private father-visit/right-to-exist disclosure in the reviewed envelope.

### EPI-N25-ENA-E0026 - verbal inaccessibility versus deliberate withholding

Ena learns/strengthens that:

- Mafuyu may need pressure/time to turn weak feeling into first-person language, but the limitation is not simply unwillingness;
- Ena's own envy can contaminate how she pushes Mafuyu and should be owned explicitly;
- Mizuki is difficult in a different way: Ena believes more could be said, but Mizuki deliberately deflects;
- ordinary contact can improve Mizuki's visible state without revealing the guarded cause.

Ena still does not know Mizuki's guarded content or the full Kanade-Mizuki private disclosure.

### EPI-N25-LUKA-E0026 - direct questioning with explicit non-omniscience

N25 Luka knows that the Empty SEKAI has changed relationally and that Kanade's initial composition lacks an articulated destination. She can observe Mafuyu hesitating around introspection, but explicitly concludes that she still does not know the answer. Her method is repeated questioning and salience retention, not authoritative decoding.

### EPI-N25-G-E0026 - unequal knowledge expands

The group shares the new song, Mafuyu's smile/warmth, and ordinary support/input behavior. The group does **not** become epistemically uniform. Mizuki possesses unusually private Kanade guilt information; Ena separately recognizes Mizuki withholding; Mafuyu lacks the private Kanade disclosure; Luka's hypotheses remain manifestation-specific.

**Authority statement:** EVENT_0026 deepens mutual support while preserving strong directional knowledge asymmetry.
## 8.8 EVENT_0029 knowledge-state integration

### EPI-N25-EMZ-E0029 - concern becomes method knowledge without guarded-content access

Ena directly knows/strengthens that:

- Mizuki's unresolved problem persists despite ordinary social fluency;
- repeated direct concern has not produced disclosure;
- low-pressure outings can coincide with visible improvement;
- therefore “nothing helps” is too strong, even when the cause remains unknown;
- a long campaign may be required;
- repeated invitations can themselves become intrusive enough to monitor.

Mizuki directly knows/strengthens that:

- Ena has been worrying and adjusting ordinary contact partly because of that concern;
- Airi believes Ena can be relied upon in trouble;
- Akito independently describes reciprocal inconvenience/reliance with Ena as legitimate and credits N25 with helping Ena through an earlier crisis;
- Ena continues inviting Mizuki without making disclosure the price of contact.

**Constraint:** neither direction acquires the guarded content. Increased evidence of relational safety is not equivalent to completed disclosure.

### EPI-N25-MF-MODE-E0029 - public/private mode contrast becomes shared observer knowledge

Ena and Mizuki directly observe Mafuyu switch into polished public first-meeting behavior with Akito and recognize that this differs from the candid/blunt mode familiar inside N25. Ena privately judges the candid mode likely less stressful for Mafuyu.

**Constraint:** this does not give Ena/Mizuki the audience's complete developmental account of Mafuyu's good-girl structure.

Akito receives only the public presentation plus the bounded fact that Mafuyu is associated with Ena's creative circle. Do not import N25 crisis knowledge into Akito.

### EPI-N25-MZ-TOYA-E0029 - bounded structural-comparison knowledge

Mizuki learns that Toya experienced highly controlled classical training, later left that path, now genuinely enjoys chosen singing, and has been able to face his father somewhat with support from companions.

**Analytical truth status:** useful structural comparison, not identical-problem evidence. Mizuki does not receive Toya's complete biography and Toya's trajectory does not predict Mizuki's disclosure outcome.

### EPI-N25-G-E0029 - ordinary-world knowledge expands without private-history collapse

The group gains ordinary public-event knowledge: Akito performs music, Ena/Akito are visibly abrasive but practically connected siblings, Kanade learns from live sound, Mafuyu participates in games and reports loudness effects, and everyone accumulates shared festival memory. The group can later recount that memory to N25 Miku.

**Authority statement:** EVENT_0029 increases ordinary mutual knowledge while preserving the major private asymmetry around Mizuki's guarded issue and Mafuyu/Kanade audience-richer histories.

## 8.9 EVENT_0032 knowledge-state integration

### EPI-N25-KM-E0032 - aquarium maintenance as weak preference evidence

Kanade directly observes that Mafuyu is voluntarily replacing aquarium seagrass. Mafuyu explicitly cannot say whether she likes the aquarium. Kanade infers that total lack of interest would make the maintenance behavior less likely.

- **Kanade knowledge class:** `INFERENCE_WEAK` about possible Mafuyu interest/salience.
- **Mafuyu knowledge class:** `OPEN` categorical preference; direct knowledge only that she is maintaining the aquarium.
- **Guardrail:** behavior can be evidence without becoming conclusive external authorship of preference.

### EPI-N25-KMZ-E0032 - ordinary bodily-state monitoring

Mizuki directly observes Kanade's discomfort/pallor outdoors, asks whether Kanade is ill, and learns that bright sunlight/eye adjustment is the immediate problem.

This is ordinary direct knowledge about Kanade's bodily/environmental tolerance, not new access to Kanade's guilt structure.

## 8.10 EVENT_0033 knowledge-state integration

### EPI-N25-MF-MOON-E0033 - weak first-person non-null affect without categorical preference

Mafuyu initially predicts that looking at the moon will probably produce no feeling. During the shared activity she directly experiences enough non-null response to say that looking is not boring and privately register that the experience is not unpleasant.

- **Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_WITNESS` by Mafuyu of her own weak valence; analytically `DIRECT_EXPERIENCE / WEAK_SELF_EVIDENCE`.
- **Not learned:** “I like moon-viewing” remains unsupported.

Kanade, Ena, and Mizuki directly witness Mafuyu's participation and hear the low-intensity public-in-Nightcord evaluation. They may know the experience was not wholly affectively null. They do not gain categorical certainty about a stable preference.

### EPI-CROSS-MAFUYU-SHIZUKU-E0033 - public-mode-only knowledge boundary

Shizuku directly knows Mafuyu as a capable kyudo/school peer who listens, gives useful suggestions, notices ordinary details, and responds acceptingly to unusual habits. Shizuku does **not** receive Mafuyu's N25 crisis history, Empty SEKAI knowledge, or private moon-viewing response.

Mafuyu directly knows that her seasonal suggestions helped Shizuku/MMJ devise a livestream concept and learns ordinary Shizuku habits/preferences. Mafuyu does not thereby receive the full private Hinomori-family memory structure driving the event.

### EPI-N25-G-E0033 - distributed ritual without epistemic collapse

The group learns that a shared ordinary ritual can be enacted while physically separated, but no private-history asymmetry is erased. Mizuki's guarded issue remains undisclosed; Shizuku/MMJ remain outside N25-private knowledge; audience knowledge of Mafuyu's public/private contrast remains richer than any single external observer's knowledge.

## 8.11 EVENT_0034 knowledge-state integration

### EPI-CROSS-KANADE-HONAMI-E0034 - bounded rescue update and reciprocal-support knowledge

Through card `PJSK:card:0281:02`, Honami directly learns that:

- Kanade's anonymized “person she wants to help” has not changed dramatically overall;
- a recent Kanade song nevertheless reached that person **a little**;
- Kanade treats that partial effect as meaningful enough to revise/generalize how she thinks about composing for a listener;
- Kanade herself struggles, doubts, and hits creative walls rather than producing successful songs effortlessly;
- Kanade explicitly experiences Honami's kindness and continued presence as meaningful support.

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_DISCLOSURE` for Kanade's statements; `INFERENCE_STRONG` for Honami's corrected picture of Kanade as a struggling rather than effortless creator.

Honami still does **not** learn:

- that the person is Mafuyu;
- Mafuyu's identity-search/disappearance crisis in specific terms;
- the Empty SEKAI/N25 Virtual Singer structure;
- Kanade's full father/guilt/penance history;
- the current internal state of N25 relationships.

Kanade directly learns that Honami's band has begun performing an original song and receives a recording. Her recognition of the vocalist as possibly familiar remains unresolved; the source supports only weak familiarity, not identification.

**Epistemic guardrail:** increased reciprocal disclosure does not collapse the professional/privacy boundary or authorize Honami to reason with N25-private facts.

## 8.12 EVENT_0035 knowledge-state integration

### EPI-N25-MF-E0035 - autobiographical affect knowledge without categorical naming

Mafuyu directly learns through experience that:

- the warm chest feeling produced by Kanade's recent song can recur and can be associated with a specific childhood care memory;
- a positive feeling can be **felt** (`感じられる`) even while its categorical name and complete causal meaning remain unavailable;
- wanting to preserve a feeling is itself first-person evidence even when explanation is incomplete;
- her own wording can be iteratively tested through lyric and performance until it better matches what she wants to communicate;
- childhood memory can contain genuine self-originated preference/attachment evidence (rabbit-shaped apple, desire for maternal presence/hand-holding) without automatically resolving the current family relationship.

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_EXPERIENCE + SELF_KNOWLEDGE`, with interpretation deliberately incomplete.

**Still unknown/open:** a comprehensive name for the recovered feeling, a broad ordinary preference map, a complete causal account of her identity erosion, and any conclusion that the present family conflict is resolved.

### EPI-N25-K-E0035 - Kanade sees positive reach but not Mafuyu's full internal memory

Kanade directly knows that Mafuyu is investigating the warmth/smile produced by her song, that Mafuyu became ill under severe overwork, and that concrete care can calm her enough to rest. Kanade directly discloses that the song drew on positive memories of her own parents and on the wish for Mafuyu to smile genuinely. By the group lyric review, Kanade also learns Mafuyu associates the song's feeling with an old apple-related memory and has managed to preserve something important in lyrics.

**Boundary:** Kanade does **not** thereby gain the audience's full access to the childhood rabbit-apple/hand-holding sequence or a definitive label for Mafuyu's feeling. `positive reach observed` is not `internal state fully known`.

### EPI-N25-G-E0035 - group recognizes qualitative Mafuyu authorship

Kanade, Ena, and Mizuki directly learn that:

- Mafuyu has, for the first time in their shared production history, described the lyrics as what she herself wanted to write;
- the lyrics carry both pain and a warm orientation toward someone;
- Mafuyu associates the affect with an old apple-related memory;
- Mafuyu still cannot explain the feeling beyond that and does not claim categorical certainty.

Ena's observation that this is the first time she has heard Mafuyu say she wrote what she wanted is direct group evidence of changed first-person authorship. The group does not become omniscient about Mafuyu's family history.

### EPI-N25-MZ-E0035 - progress comparison strengthens disclosure-conflict self-knowledge

Mizuki directly witnesses Mafuyu's active search and later concludes that Mafuyu, Kanade, and Ena appear to be moving forward while Mizuki feels stuck. This is strong self-knowledge about the **existence** of the disclosure conflict, not readiness to disclose its guarded content.

### EPI-N25-MIKU-E0035 - bounded co-memory and confidentiality

N25 Miku directly learns that Mafuyu wants help testing whether lyric wording carries a particular feeling and later promises to remember the recovered trace together with Mafuyu. Separately, Miku learns that Mizuki is distressed and does not want Kanade/Ena told. Miku agrees to keep that boundary.

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_DISCLOSURE + DIRECT_WITNESS`.

**Constraint:** Miku does not know the full content of Mizuki's guarded issue and does not become a transparent reader of either human's mind.

### EPI-CROSS-SHIZUKU-MF-E0035 - fatigue visibility without private-history access

Shizuku directly observes Mafuyu's fatigue/overcommitment and notices difficulty answering a question about relaxation or being her natural self. Mafuyu supplies a socially polished response. Shizuku therefore has stronger evidence that Mafuyu is overworked, but still lacks Nightcord crisis, Empty SEKAI, and family identity-search knowledge.

### Audience/participant separation strengthened

The audience directly witnesses the detailed childhood fever memory and current maternal-expectation scenes. Nightcord receives only the bounded account Mafuyu chooses to communicate. Do not leak the full memory transcript backward into Kanade/Ena/Mizuki reasoning.

## 8.13 EVENT_0036 knowledge-state integration

### EPI-N25-G-E0036 - direct observation of Mafuyu mode switching and differentiated musical response

The N25 humans directly observe Mafuyu shifting into a bright, socially optimized school register around Shizuku/Airi/Haruka and returning to her usual private N25 register after those peers leave. Ena and Mizuki explicitly notice the contrast.

Later, the group receives Mafuyu's direct report that none of the Fan Festa music produced a felt response, even though Minori previously received polished public praise from Mafuyu. The group therefore has unusually clean evidence that public prosocial appraisal and private affective investment are not equivalent.

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_WITNESS + DIRECT_DISCLOSURE`.

**Constraint:** N25 may know the mode contrast; this does not make every public Mafuyu statement false or permit members to infer hidden feeling without evidence.

### EPI-N25-MF-E0036 - creative artifact as memory-retention evidence

Mafuyu directly learns that the collaborative song built from her EVENT_0035 lyrics can function as an external memory support. She says that with this song, she thinks she will not forget the feeling she remembered.

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_EXPERIENTIAL_EVIDENCE` within the ledger's direct-evidence logic.

**Constraint:** retention of one recovered feeling is not a complete preference map or full autobiographical integration.

### EPI-CROSS-WXS-MZ-E0036 - bounded Mizuki background transmission

In Tsukasa card `0293:01`, Rui tells Emu, Tsukasa, and Nene that Akiyama Mizuki is his middle-school acquaintance, a Kamiyama first-year, and the person who made Rui/Nene/Nene-Robo's costumes. Emu learns these facts for the first time; Tsukasa and Nene explicitly react to the costume-maker fact as new information.

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_DISCLOSURE` from Rui.

**Prohibited leakage:** this transmission does not include Mizuki's guarded issue, N25 membership/private crisis history, disclosure conflict, or Empty SEKAI knowledge.

### Other bounded cross-unit knowledge

Airi, Shizuku, and Haruka learn that Kanade belongs to the same creative circle as Mafuyu/Ena/Mizuki; Minori later meets all four. Kanade recognizes Ichika's singing voice from prior contact. Preserve these as bounded contact facts until the relevant other-unit foundations are analyzed.

## 8.14 EVENT_0039 knowledge-state integration

### EPI-N25-MZ-E0039 - attachment, exposure risk, and waiting become direct self-knowledge

Mizuki directly knows that:

- N25 has become important enough that possible exposure produces acute fear of relational change;
- the problem is not simply expectation of cruelty: Mizuki expects the members may be kind and still fears that kindness may reorganize the relationship;
- social-network overlap makes accidental/indirect exposure more plausible;
- nondisclosure increasingly feels like deception toward people Mizuki values;
- Ena explicitly considers Mizuki a friend and promises continued presence without immediate disclosure;
- that promise reduces immediate pressure but also makes continued deferral a viable way to preserve the present relationship;
- Mizuki's stated “someday, when I can tell you” trajectory is privately recognized as not fully honest at this endpoint.

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_SELF_KNOWLEDGE + DIRECT_DISCLOSURE_FROM_ENA`.

**Constraint:** this does not establish that Mizuki has decided never to disclose. It establishes current ambivalence and a consciously available deferral motive.

### EPI-N25-ENA-MZ-E0039 - major undisclosed issue confirmed; content remains unavailable

Ena directly learns/observes that:

- Mizuki has a significant unresolved problem;
- Mizuki can remain cheerful and enjoy ordinary activity while that problem persists;
- direct pressure to explain can cause sharp refusal/flight;
- Mizuki is not ready to disclose;
- talking is not universally beneficial, per MEIKO's bounded advice;
- waiting and continued friendship are available support strategies.

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_WITNESS + DIRECT_DISCLOSURE + INFERENCE_STRONG`.

**Still unknown:** the guarded content, its complete history, and the exact feared social consequence.

**Behavioral consequence:** Ena explicitly stops making disclosure the price of care and adopts a waiting commitment.

### EPI-CROSS-RUI-MZ-E0039 - waiting structure and loneliness-history become directly shared

Rui directly learns that Mizuki describes themself as possibly becoming `待たれる側`. He can infer that a valued relationship now contains unresolved waiting, but Mizuki does not disclose the guarded issue.

Mizuki directly learns that Rui sees their bond as no longer dependent on current shared isolation: they may not still be `孤独な仲間`, but Rui considers them `孤独を知る仲間` and affirms Mizuki's persistence in being themself.

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_DISCLOSURE + INFERENCE_STRONG`.

**EVENT_0038 guardrail preserved:** Mizuki does not thereby acquire the detailed Rui childhood scenes previously classified `AUDIENCE_RICHER`.

### EPI-N25-MEIKO-E0039 - bounded support-ethics inference without secret access

MEIKO knows/strongly infers that:

- Mizuki is under meaningful pressure connected to important relationships;
- Ena's wish to help is sincere and persistent;
- forced or premature speech can create harm;
- different supporters may legitimately use different methods.

MEIKO still does **not** know or reveal the guarded content. Card `0310` confirms that her advice is based on bounded observation/inference rather than authoritative decoding.

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_WITNESS + INFERENCE_STRONG`; content remains `OPEN/UNKNOWN`.

### EPI-N25-MF-MZ-E0039 - progress question without causal disclosure

Mafuyu directly hears Mizuki ask whether moving forward through painful self-recovery is frightening and answers from her own experience. Mafuyu notices the question is unusual enough to invite follow-up, but the conversation is interrupted.

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_WITNESS`; causal meaning `OPEN`.

**Constraint:** Mafuyu does not learn Mizuki's guarded issue or the Ena rooftop conversation.

### EPI-N25-K-E0039 - broader recipient evidence without completed-salvation inference

Kanade directly observes strong listener response to the current N25 song and can cautiously infer that the song reached people beyond Mafuyu. At the hospital she also thinks her father may have smiled, but explicitly preserves uncertainty (`気のせい` remains possible).

**Knowledge class:** public response `DIRECT_WITNESS`; father smile `INFERENCE_WEAK/OPEN`.

**Constraint:** do not promote the possible smile into certain recovery evidence or treat listener reach as completed salvation.

### EPI-CROSS-VBS-N25-E0039 - bounded acquaintance-network overlap

The family-restaurant encounter and later N25 chatter establish bounded cross-unit identity mapping among Mizuki/Ena/Kanade/Mafuyu and An/Kohane/Akito/Toya. Participants learn ordinary acquaintance, sibling, school, and performer facts.

**Constraint:** no source in the complete EVENT_0039 envelope transmits Mizuki's guarded issue, N25's Empty-SEKAI crisis history, or other private N25 causal biographies to VBS, and no VBS member supplies the guarded content to N25.

### Group epistemic result

N25's overall information asymmetry remains substantial. Ena now knows more about the **existence and support boundary** of Mizuki's problem, but Kanade and Mafuyu do not receive the guarded content; Rui and MEIKO receive only bounded structural information. EVENT_0039 therefore increases relational knowledge while preserving the central secret.

## 8.15 EVENT_0041 knowledge-state integration - ordinary Mizuki-An knowledge without guarded-content leakage

### `EPI-CROSS-AN-MZ-E0007` - EVENT_0041 extension

Card `0322:01-02` adds low-stakes mutual knowledge inside the already established partial-disclosure friendship:

- Mizuki knows An's attachment to Kohane well enough to identify Kohane immediately as the likely `date` partner and treats that knowledge as ordinary teasing material.
- An learns/receives Mizuki's person-specific fashion judgment: a chic variant from the new shop is predicted to suit An's straight hair.
- Mizuki learns that An considers the outing highly worth recounting and wants to continue the conversation at school; An receives Mizuki's explicit interest in hearing it.
- Both therefore have direct evidence that ordinary friendship/fashion/social conversation remains available after EVENT_0039.

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_WITNESS + DIRECT_DISCLOSURE` for ordinary preference/social facts.

**Boundary preserved:** An does **not** learn Mizuki's guarded content, the Ena waiting promise, N25's Empty-SEKAI crisis history, or other private N25 causal biography. Mizuki does not learn any hidden VBS causal history from this card. The EVENT_0007 partial-disclosure guardrail remains intact.

### `PJSK:card:0323:02` retrospective reference

Shizuku and Airi remember earlier shared outings with Ena and Mizuki. This establishes memory continuity only; because neither N25 member is present and no new proposition about them is learned, no separate epistemic transition is created.

## 8.16 EVENT_0042 knowledge-state integration - relational self-evidence and bounded cross-unit creative knowledge

### EPI-N25-MF-E0042 - relational warmth / contribution-linked first-person knowledge

Mafuyu now directly knows by first-person experience that:

- Kanade's visible happiness can coincide with warmth in Mafuyu's chest even when no song or recovered childhood memory is the immediate trigger;
- a small action of Mafuyu's can help produce another person's positive state, and recognizing that contribution can itself feel warm;
- the warmth can be treated as real and non-negative before Mafuyu knows its categorical meaning;
- going to an ordinary mixed-unit meeting can be chosen as a way to investigate that feeling;
- recent lyrics contain more first-person intention than the strongest `I only fit words to Kanade's music` account allows: she wanted to preserve her feeling and recognizes that being heard by someone is preferable to no one;
- N25 Miku is relationally salient enough for Mafuyu to ask what Miku is to her, even though Mafuyu still cannot answer the question.

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_WITNESS + FIRST_PERSON_EXPERIENTIAL_KNOWLEDGE + PARTIAL_SELF_INFERENCE`.  
**Constraint:** do not upgrade the evidence into a settled label such as friendship/love/altruism or broad preference recovery.

### EPI-N25-K-E0042 - ordinary-life loss becomes consciously available

Kanade directly learns/recognizes that:

- ordinary music/social conversation with peers outside N25 can be enjoyable without rescue utility;
- she remembers childhood enjoyment with friends that had become inaccessible to her ordinary self-model;
- years dominated by `music must save people` may have coincided with forgetting/losing ordinary-life domains;
- noticing that possibility can itself be regarded as good.

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_WITNESS + AUTOBIOGRAPHICAL_RECALL + PARTIAL_SELF_INFERENCE`.  
**Constraint:** Kanade has not concluded that rescue/penance is wrong, has not corrected the father-guilt model, and has not adopted a healthy self-care plan.

### EPI-N25-MIKU-E0042 - Mafuyu warmth, promise, and sensory-limit knowledge

N25 Miku directly learns that:

- Mafuyu experienced unexplained warmth around the mixed-event encounter and later around snow;
- Mafuyu does not yet know what the warmth means;
- Mafuyu will remember and act on an ordinary wish Miku expresses, as shown by the snow promise and forecast follow-through;
- Miku herself can experience a relational analogue of warmth from Mafuyu's effort even though she cannot physically feel the external cold;
- taste/sensory retrieval can be scaffolded by Miku describing flavors, with Luka later joining.

**Constraint:** Miku remains non-omniscient and physically manifestation-limited. She cannot directly retrieve Mafuyu's internal state or substitute description for Mafuyu's own sensation.

### EPI-CROSS-ICHIKA-N25-E0042 - public creative-identity knowledge only

Ichika directly learns that:

- Kanade is the creator she met at the CD shop and is a composer in Mafuyu's music circle;
- Mafuyu writes lyrics for the publicly shared N25 work she hears;
- Kanade and Mafuyu have close enough creative familiarity to discuss one another's work and ordinary habits;
- Mafuyu can give sophisticated lyric-craft explanations.

**Boundary:** Ichika does **not** learn Snow/OWN identity history, Empty SEKAI, disappearance crisis, Kanade's father/guilt history, Mafuyu's family/autonomy conflict, Mizuki's guarded issue, or the private autobiographical referent of Mafuyu's recent lyrics.

### EPI-CROSS-HONAMI-N25-E0042 - domestic bridge plus public creative overlap

Honami directly learns/observes more of Kanade and Mafuyu's public creative collaboration and the new Kanade-Ichika contact. She already knows Kanade through domestic work and preserves that channel's confidentiality.

**Boundary:** practical/domestic access does not authorize N25-private causal knowledge. Honami remains a bounded support/social bridge, not an N25 crisis confidant.

### Group epistemic result

Cross-unit network overlap increases, but information remains partitioned. EVENT_0042 demonstrates that characters can become more socially connected **without** flattening audience-only/private N25 knowledge into shared participant knowledge.


## 8.17 EVENT_0044 knowledge-state integration - bounded Shinonome family memory and safety transmission

### `EPI-FAMILY-AKITO-ENA-E0044` - remembered family history / practical risk knowledge

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_WITNESS + TRANSMISSION_ESTABLISHED_SOURCE_UNSPECIFIED`.

Akito directly remembers from childhood that:

- he, Ena, and their mother spent ordinary leisure time together on a family camping trip;
- their father continued painting during much of the trip;
- Ena became angry that their father was not engaging with the family and tried to interrupt that painter-role absorption;
- this is, in Akito's recollection, the last family camping trip and still his most enjoyable camping memory.

Separately, core `0044:06` establishes that Akito **has heard** that Ena once had serious trouble in the mountains and uses that information to check cellular/GPS conditions before his own outing.

**Transmission guardrail:** the source establishes that the information reached Akito, but does not identify who told him or how much of Ena's mountain incident he knows. Do not assume Ena personally disclosed it, that Akito knows its full circumstances, or that he possesses unrelated N25-private information.

### `EPI-CROSS-VBS-ENA-E0044` - bounded childhood-family anecdote

Through Akito's card `0342:01`, Kohane, An, and Toya directly receive a limited childhood anecdote about Ena and the Shinonome family: the siblings camped with their parents, their father spent substantial time painting, Ena protested the lack of family engagement, and Akito remembers the trip positively.

**Constraint:** this does not give VBS Ena's N25 crisis history, artistic-recognition history in full, current N25 relationships, or any private Nightcord/SEKAI knowledge.

### Epistemic result

EVENT_0044 adds family-history knowledge but no N25-private leakage. No Mafuyu, Kanade, Mizuki, or N25-group epistemic state changes. An receives no new Mizuki knowledge, so `EPI-CROSS-AN-MZ-E0007` remains unchanged.

## 8.18 EVENT_0045 knowledge-state integration - ritual knowledge without prayer-thought leakage

### `EPI-N25-G-E0045` - second-year ordinary ritual becomes shared group knowledge

All four N25 humans directly learn that another New Year can be organized as voluntary shared time outside production/crisis. They know Mafuyu has a family daytime plan and is willing to ask to join in the evening; they later directly experience the shrine visit and subsequent ordinary social planning.

### `EPI-N25-KM-E0045` - shared omikuji / direct recommitment

Kanade and Mafuyu directly know they drew the same `末吉` result and the same `願望 - 努力すれば叶う` line. Mafuyu directly hears Kanade say she will keep trying this year. This does **not** grant Mafuyu the exact content of Kanade's subsequent silent prayer unless a later source transmits it.

### `EPI-N25-AUD-E0045` - exact prayer contents are audience-richer

The following are internal prayer thoughts shown to the audience:

- Ena wants to make art she herself can accept and privately reaffirms waiting for Mizuki;
- Mizuki wants the current shared time to continue;
- Mafuyu wants to find herself;
- Kanade wants to make music that saves everyone.

The other N25 humans do not automatically know these exact formulations. N25 Miku observes that everyone is praying seriously and wishes for their wishes to come true, but the source does not establish mind-reading of their contents.

### `EPI-N25-MIKU-E0045` - participant-side SEKAI and companionship knowledge

Through `shuffle_15_017-018`, Miku explicitly recognizes that Mafuyu has changed enough that the Empty SEKAI is no longer literally unpopulated, while also preserving two identity propositions: it remains a SEKAI generated from Mafuyu's feelings, and she remains `this SEKAI's Miku`. She also directly recognizes that seeing Rin/Luka/MEIKO makes her happy and chooses contact with them.

### Bounded cross-unit ordinary knowledge

- `monthly2112_003`: Ena and Airi exchange public MMJ-stream/wardrobe ideas; Ena intends to tell Mizuki if the idea is adopted.
- `monthly2112_004`: Mizuki and Shizuku share direct knowledge of Mizuki's sewing/embroidery competence.
- `0045:09`: Shizuku/MMJ know only that the N25 group has come for an ordinary shrine visit; no crisis/SEKAI/private biography is transmitted.
- `monthly2112_008`: N25 Miku can pass a ribbon-tying technique learned from Mizuki to N25 Rin; this is bounded practical knowledge, not broad biographical transmission.


## 8.19 EVENT_0047 knowledge-state integration - origin history without flashback leakage

### `EPI-N25-K-E0047` - rescue-origin reflexivity and sub-salvation efficacy

Kanade directly recognizes/learns in the present that:

- she is calmer than in the pre-N25 period and trusted people may be part of that change;
- the first K/Snow song can have value by remaining beside a listener even if it does not qualify as complete salvation;
- ordinary four-person N25 meals/creation have become normal enough to be noticed as a large change;
- Honami's care is something Kanade wants to reciprocate rather than merely consume.

The flashback also gives the audience direct causal evidence that rescue obligation, life-qualification logic, and severe bodily neglect predate Snow. Do not automatically treat every flashback internal sentence as later group knowledge.

### `EPI-N25-MF-E0047` - selective autobiographical continuity and explicit relational appraisal

Mafuyu directly recognizes that:

- an old threatening-creature self-description no longer fits, although she cannot name a replacement;
- practical concern for Kanade may be `worry`; she treats Ena/Mizuki's interpretation as a hypothesis and then continues the care behavior;
- most ordinary school memory from two years earlier is largely blank, while K-song/creative-origin memories became recoverable during the retelling;
- meeting Kanade, making music, and meeting Ena/Mizuki/Miku were, in aggregate, good;
- the Empty SEKAI can be used for rest and low-demand relational presence, not only acute crisis/search.

**Constraint:** Mafuyu still does not know why the memory distribution differs, does not recover broad ordinary preference, and does not receive an externally authoritative emotional label.

### `EPI-N25-G-E0047` - bounded shared origin-story knowledge

Ena and Mizuki directly receive a much richer origin account than they previously possessed. Later card/area dialogue confirms that they know Kanade collapsed in the pre-N25 period, that the household-service person who found her became Honami, that K/Snow began through music under severe distress, and that the relationship has changed substantially since then.

**Critical leakage guardrail:** the audience sees detailed internal flashback monologue. The group does not automatically inherit every exact private thought, bodily sensation, graduation-gift reflection, or unspoken hope unless present dialogue/card material separately transmits it.

### `EPI-N25-VS-E0047` - narrated origin history and SEKAI memory assurance

Miku/Luka hear the origin framing in the present, and card `0365` has the story retold to Rin/MEIKO. Miku additionally hears Mafuyu's selective-memory concern and responds with a manifestation-specific continuity claim: once-born feelings may be forgotten or change, but Miku/SEKAI remember Mafuyu's feelings.

**Constraint:** treat this as in-world relational/ontological knowledge, not as proof that Virtual Singers can query a perfect historical archive or read current minds.

### `EPI-CROSS-HONAMI-N25-E0042` - EVENT_0047 historical extension

Preserve the existing cross-unit knowledge ID while adding direct history: Honami herself witnessed/found Kanade's collapse before the continuing domestic-support relationship formed. N25 later learns this bounded fact through Kanade's account. Honami's practical access still does not grant her N25 crisis/SEKAI/private biography as a whole.

### Deferred Airi/Akito routing knowledge

Card `0364:01` establishes for the audience/Ena retrospective model that seeing Airi pursue her idol dream helped Ena restart effort after discouragement, and that Ena's mother knew Airi was Ena's friend through Akito. These are routing-grade MMJ/VBS cross-unit facts. Do not infer broader unit knowledge or current relationship state before the relevant foundations are analyzed.

### `EPI-CROSS-AN-MZ-E0007` - EVENT_0051 extension

Mizuki directly experiences reciprocal White Day exchange and privately recognizes that An's continuing ordinary conversation is deeply welcome. This is **direct self-knowledge** about the value of the relationship, not disclosure to An about why such normality matters.

EVENT_0051 adds direct/self-inferred knowledge that Mizuki can:

- initiate ordinary outings and recipient-specific care while disclosure remains deferred;
- function effectively in public improvisation despite nervousness;
- value safe normality as a relationship property;
- re-motivate a disliked school obligation when another person's burden becomes salient.

The guarded content itself remains unavailable to outsiders and is not newly disclosed to N25.

Kanade, Mafuyu, and Ena directly receive distinct White Day gifts from Mizuki and therefore know that Mizuki invested recipient-specific thought in the exchange. Kanade tentatively infers from repeated behavior that her usual cup-noodle flavor may count as a favorite and tells Rin enough for Rin to understand the bounded preference. Kanade then offers to share samples.

Mafuyu directly tracks whether Luka has finished the current textbook page before turning it. Luka directly experiences the accommodation. Ena observes that Mafuyu's behavior is more considerate than a literal `I do not mind` reading alone would predict.

**Knowledge class:** Mafuyu `DIRECT_BEHAVIORAL_CHOICE`; Luka `DIRECT_WITNESS`; Ena `DIRECT_WITNESS + INFERENCE_BOUNDED`.

Akito, Tsukasa, Haruka, and other event participants gain only ordinary public/social knowledge of Mizuki. None receives the guarded content or N25-private causal history.

## 8.21 EVENT_0052 knowledge-state integration - craft competence becomes bounded cross-unit knowledge

Shizuku directly knows that Mizuki has useful collage/material knowledge and can distinguish garment materials at a level sufficient for practical costume advice. Through the consultation and later technical assistance, Shizuku also knows that Mizuki can locate useful garment-design resources and assist with generated pattern adjustment. She therefore treats Mizuki as a reliable practical collaborator for the MMJ costume project.

Mizuki directly learns that Shizuku is designing MMJ stage costumes with fan participation, that material choice and pattern preparation are live production problems, and that Shizuku is taking responsibility for translating design intent into manufacturable guidance. Mizuki's explicit MMJ fandom makes participation personally enjoyable as well as practically useful.

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_DISCLOSURE + DIRECT_PARTICIPATION`.

### MMJ-side bounded knowledge of Mizuki

By card `0395:02`, Airi/Haruka/Minori receive bounded group knowledge that Mizuki helped Shizuku with material resources, useful sites, and pattern-generation/adjustment work. Airi also recognizes that this labor deserves thanks. This does not grant the MMJ members broader N25-private knowledge.

### Cross-unit boundary

No EVENT_0052 participant receives Mizuki's guarded content, the Ena waiting structure, or N25 crisis/SEKAI biography. `REL-CROSS-MAFUYU-SHIZUKU-E0033` receives no new epistemic extension because Mafuyu does not participate in EVENT_0052.

## 9. Prohibited backward leakage examples

Do not allow:

- early Ena to understand the full family mechanism behind Mafuyu;
- early Kanade to know Mafuyu wanted to become a nurse;
- Mafuyu to know the objective multicausal structure of Kanade's father's collapse rather than Kanade's guilt narrative;
- Mizuki or Ena to cite audience-only sensory-flattening evidence without later disclosure;
- post-main characters to reference event/card information that has not yet been integrated;
- a character to know another character's internal monologue unless the source establishes later disclosure.

## 10. Future-event knowledge transition template

```yaml
knowledge_transition_id: <stable-id>
character: <name>
source_locator: <event/card/area/etc>
previous_knowledge_state: <id>
proposition: <what is learned/believed>
knowledge_class: DIRECT_WITNESS | DIRECT_DISCLOSURE | INFERENCE_STRONG | INFERENCE_WEAK | BELIEF_INCORRECT_OR_DISTORTED | UNKNOWN_TRANSMISSION
source_of_information: <speaker/event/observation>
confidence_held_by_character: high | medium | low
analytical_truth_status: supported | partial | mistaken | open
relationship_effect: ...
behavioral_effect: ...
new_knowledge_state: <id or no-global-state-change>
notes: ...
```
