- Virtual Singer manifestation-specific knowledge: `PENDING_CONTEXT_MODEL`
- N25 disclosures after EVENT_0053: `PENDING_EVENT_INTEGRATION`
- cross-unit knowledge propagation beyond explicitly bounded EVENT_0004, EVENT_0007, EVENT_0019, EVENT_0026, EVENT_0033, EVENT_0034, EVENT_0035, EVENT_0036, EVENT_0038, EVENT_0039, EVENT_0041, EVENT_0042, EVENT_0051, and EVENT_0052 entries: `PENDING_CROSS_UNIT_ANALYSIS`; bounded entries do not authorize broad cross-unit synthesis


## 8.24 EVENT_0053 knowledge-state integration

### EPI-N25-E-E0053 — Ena artistic deficit / continuation knowledge

Ena directly learns/accepts that her current fundamentals are materially behind the trained peers she compares herself with and that her expressive ambition exceeds her current technical repertoire. She also directly learns that N25 members perceive concrete expressive development across the two years she initially labels as wasted. Yukihira remembers her earlier work and retained critique context, but explicitly does not guarantee future success.

**Behavioral effect:** technical weakness becomes a problem to train rather than sufficient evidence that drawing must end.

### EPI-N25-G-E0053 — group knowledge of Ena's training problem

Kanade, Mafuyu, and Mizuki learn the broad art-school history and that returning is frightening because Ena previously withdrew after painful evaluation/comparison. They know Ena has chosen to return because she wants greater expressive ability.

**Constraint:** this does not automatically grant every audience-known detail of the father's original judgment, Ena's internal monologue, or every class interaction.

### EPI-N25-K-E0053 — Kanade agency correction

Kanade initially interprets Ena's painful training choice through responsibility inflation: N25's work may have made Ena suffer. Mafuyu and Mizuki directly correct the premise, and Kanade accepts that Ena chose the path because it serves Ena's own artistic aims.

**Reconstruction consequence:** Kanade can revise guilt when it improperly converts another person's self-authored choice into Kanade-caused sacrifice.

### EPI-N25-G-CREATIVE-E0053 — reciprocal creative-process knowledge

The four humans explicitly articulate that composition, lyrics, illustration, and storyboard/MV construction can feed back into one another. Kanade can recognize intended sound through the others' work; Mafuyu can revise lyrics after seeing Ena's image; group critique can alter visual arrangement; Mizuki's MV solution can preserve material that otherwise seems excessive.

**Truth status:** direct shared production knowledge.  
**Constraint:** reciprocal influence does not erase specialized roles or prove equal causal weight in every song.

### EPI-N25-RIN-E0053 — pain/agency distinction

N25 Rin learns that Ena's chosen development may entail continuing pain. Rin's desire that Ena not suffer does not become authority to stop Ena's chosen path; Miku/MEIKO/Luka help preserve the distinction between care and control.

## 8.25 EVENT_0054 epistemic integration — Miku self-knowledge and exceptional cross-SEKAI adjacency

### EPI-N25-MIKU-E0054 — relational happiness / prosocial intention

N25 Miku directly learns that the presence of Mafuyu, Kanade, Ena, Mizuki, and the other N25 manifestations can make her chest feel warm and can be consciously interpreted as happiness. She further recognizes a first-person desire to keep doing things for them.

This is self-knowledge, not privileged knowledge of the humans. Preserve Miku's established non-omniscience.

### EPI-PJSK-SEKAI-E0054 — bounded cross-SEKAI permeability

Selected Virtual Singer manifestations directly or strongly infer that:

- `想いの欠片` can represent thoughts that did not themselves form a SEKAI;
- fragments can drift into established SEKAI and disclose partial thought content;
- an exceptional fragment/event can place manifestations from separate SEKAI into a temporary perceptual adjacency;
- the participants briefly perceive versions of familiar Virtual Singers that seem to belong to other SEKAI.

**Knowledge status:** `DIRECT_WITNESS + INFERENCE_STRONG`, but mechanistically incomplete. Participants repeatedly question whether they mis-saw the encounter and do not possess a repeatable access method.

### Human knowledge guardrail

The EVENT_0054 envelope does not establish that N25's humans learn the complete cross-SEKAI encounter. Do not give Kanade, Mafuyu, Ena, or Mizuki knowledge of the other units' SEKAI manifestations or human biographies merely because the audience sees the connective field.


## 8.26 EVENT_0058 knowledge-state integration — school enjoyment, calibrated disclosure safety, and bounded creative help

### `EPI-N25-MZ-E0058` — first-person knowledge of chosen school enjoyment

Mizuki directly knows that cheer-squad selection was initially unwanted and partly reframed through attendance incentives, but later admits that the activity itself became genuinely fun. This is participant-authoritative evidence that school-related discomfort and authentic enjoyment can coexist by context.

### `EPI-CROSS-RUI-MZ-E0058` — reciprocal outsider/safety inference

Rui explicitly tells Mizuki that an old rejection fear is stopping him from presenting what he really wants to make. Mizuki therefore possesses direct knowledge of the fear in this local production context. Mizuki's encouragement is based on observed current trust and role legitimacy; it does not grant either party general knowledge that all disclosures are safe.

### `EPI-N25-G-E0058` — bounded school-project knowledge

Kanade learns only enough about Mizuki's personal cheer project to supply an optional music lead. Later Kanade, Mafuyu, and Ena learn bounded ordinary facts about Mizuki's school participation, fatigue, enjoyment, and after-event sociality. They do **not** receive the guarded issue through this event, and cheer-group participants receive no N25-private crisis/SEKAI history.

### Knowledge guardrail

Ena's observation that Mizuki looked genuinely happy is valid evidence about outward behavior and later converges with Mizuki's own report. It is not authority over Mizuki's undisclosed interior content.

## 8.27 EVENT_0061 knowledge-state integration — family/music conflict, warmth context, and Len arrival

### `EPI-N25-G-MAFUYU-FAMILY-E0061`

Kanade, Ena, and Mizuki now directly know that Mafuyu's mother prioritizes study/future preparation over Mafuyu's music, that the synthesizer had previously almost been discarded, that Mafuyu secretly recovered it, and that current discovery/confiscation materially constrains N25 participation. They do **not** receive every audience-known childhood memory, full maternal interiority, or the complete mechanism of Mafuyu's current conflict.

### `EPI-N25-MF-E0061`

Mafuyu now has first-person evidence that the exam/expectation path can make her body feel intensely cold/ill even while she consciously calls it correct; N25/SEKAI co-presence can make the coldness recede; `here` can feel warm/relieving; and the care she receives from her mother does not feel identical to what she receives from N25. She does not yet possess a settled explanation of that difference.

### `EPI-N25-VS-LEN-E0061`

Len learns that he is not alone in Empty SEKAI, that Miku and the others are safe enough to approach gradually, and that MEIKO's distance is an intentional observational stance rather than simple rejection/loneliness. Ordinary game/social rules remain an area where his literal interpretation can surface.

### Mother-knowledge guardrail

Mafuyu's mother knows Mafuyu missed the mock exam and received a friend/rest explanation; she subsequently asks more detailed school/friend questions. She does not know N25 identities, SEKAI architecture, or the full Phoenix Wonderland decision chain.


## EVENT_0063 epistemic integration

### EPI-N25-K-E0063 — reciprocal happiness / self-permission knowledge

Kanade now directly knows that:
- ordinary recreation can be genuinely enjoyable for her;
- enjoying herself can trigger guilt when she remembers the rescue obligation;
- at least Minori explicitly wants Kanade herself to smile;
- Minori proposes that N25 likely wants the same;
- Kanade can tentatively treat that reciprocity as permission to value her own feelings somewhat more.

**Guardrail:** Kanade does not yet possess a generalized intrinsic-self-worth philosophy, and the belief that N25 wants her happiness remains partly inferential rather than a fully explicit group declaration.

### EPI-CROSS-MINORI-KANADE-E0063

Minori learns a bounded version of Kanade's mission: Kanade makes music for struggling people and treats that work as something she must do. Minori does not learn Mafuyu's identity as the central rescue target, the Empty SEKAI, Kanade's father-guilt causal history, or N25 crisis details.

### EPI-N25-G-MF-E0063 — preserve EVENT_0061 family boundary

Mafuyu's reduced work-session availability is directly observable to N25 and attributed to study/prep-school demands, while continued lyric work confirms persistence of the creative line. No new detailed family disclosure occurs.

## EVENT_0068 epistemic integration — origin history, bounded disclosure, and present retrospective knowledge

### `EPI-N25-MZ-E0068` — chosen-belonging retrospective self-knowledge

Mizuki now has explicit first-person evidence that the life produced by the earlier fearful decision to join the collaboration contains genuine present enjoyment. In card `0486`, Mizuki reviews ordinary-life photographs, recognizes that they are actually smiling/enjoying those memories, and wants to tell the older sister that the earlier encouragement mattered.

**Constraint:** recognizing present enjoyment does not answer the guarded disclosure question or establish confidence that N25 will accept the undisclosed content.

### `EPI-N25-VS-MZ-E0068` — bounded school/origin knowledge under preserved concealment

Miku and Luka receive bounded information about Mizuki's earlier school disengagement and the importance of finding K/Ena/creative collaboration. When Miku asks why school became intolerable, Mizuki gives a generalized answer rather than the guarded cause. Luka notices the answer may be preformulated from repeated questioning.

**Knowledge class:** `DIRECT_DISCLOSURE + INFERENCE_STRONG`.  
**Guardrail:** the manifestations do not gain the guarded content, audience-only flashback interiority, or authority to interpret the generic answer as complete.

### `EPI-N25-G-ORIGIN-E0068` — bounded founding creative history

The event makes the early K/Ena/Mizuki collaboration participant-relevant: Kanade's invitation, Ena/Mizuki's first cross-medium work, and the creative negotiation that followed form known relationship history. The audience receives richer moment-to-moment interiority than every present participant should automatically possess.

### `EPI-CROSS-SHIZUKU-MF-E0068` — public-mode concern / sibling-care comparison

Shizuku directly observes that Mafuyu may be unwell and offers her own sibling-care frame. Shizuku does not learn Mafuyu's family-control conflict, synth restriction, N25 identities, Empty SEKAI, or lyric-concealment history. Mafuyu receives a new relational comparison point: sibling concern can coexist with open positive attachment without carrying the same authority structure as maternal control.

### Existing EVENT_0061 family knowledge — PRESERVE

N25's concrete knowledge of Mafuyu's mother prioritizing study/future preparation over music remains bounded as established at EVENT_0061. EVENT_0068 confirms ongoing restrictions but does not disclose every audience-known home detail to the group.

## EVENT_0070 epistemic integration — Ena aesthetic authority and Honami mentoring

### `EPI-N25-E-E0070 — technical versus expressive value self-knowledge`

Ena directly learns that a work can remain technically flawed while possessing person-specific expressive value she genuinely likes. She also learns that correction can become counterproductive when it is applied before understanding what the subject/learner is trying to communicate.

**Constraint:** this does not make Ena indifferent to professional criticism or technical standards.

### `EPI-CROSS-HONAMI-ENA-E0070 — reciprocal mentoring knowledge`

Honami directly knows that Ena sees value in her distinctive drawings and will continue teaching; Ena knows Honami's prior ridicule/shame, the practical kindergarten goal, and the later positive effect of expression-preserving instruction.

### `EPI-N25-KMZ-E0070 — bounded Ena-method knowledge`

Kanade and Mizuki receive bounded participant knowledge of Ena's distinction between pure technical improvement and Honami-specific expressive value through the post-event rough-review/referral conversation. Mafuyu leaves before the fuller exchange and should not automatically inherit every detail.

## EVENT_0072 epistemic integration — Mafuyu desire and caregiving evidence

### `EPI-N25-MF-E0072 — action-specific wanting / positive-care self-evidence`

Mafuyu now directly knows that:
- other people's self-authored goals repeatedly produce a difficult-to-name salience response in her;
- useful caregiving followed by gratitude can make her chest feel lighter and coincide with spontaneous smiling;
- she can want a specific action (`少し、弾いてみたい`);
- she can want a state/activity to continue (`もう少しだけ、こうしていたい`) even while unable to categorize it as `楽しい`.

**Constraint:** she does not yet possess a settled life goal, career answer, or broad preference vocabulary.

### `EPI-N25-G-MF-E0072 — group witness of bounded positive desire`

Kanade, Ena, and Mizuki directly witness Mafuyu choose to play piano, continue the duet, and show positive affect after helping the injured child. They do not automatically know the private mother conversation, nursing-school book salience, or the fact that the volunteer choice was initially motivated by possible N25 contact.

### `EPI-CROSS-WXS-MF-E0072 — bounded desire/care knowledge`

Rui and Emu know that Mafuyu says she believes she lacks a personally wanted path, possesses practical first-aid knowledge from medical-study work, and competently helped Rui. They do not know the household control structure or the surrendered-synth history. Rui's suggestion that desire may be unrecognized remains a hypothesis rather than knowledge of Mafuyu's actual answer.

Her childhood parental memories are newly narrated history, not newly lived events. Her father’s present nonrecognition persists in her current report (`PJSK:event:0089:01:006:0002–0008`). Honami’s tea provision is recalled; Kanade directly experiences its warmth without thereby proving a general therapeutic effect (`PJSK:card:0612:02:001:0053–0061`).

### `EPI-N25-MF-E0089` — inferred exposure, unheard assertion and private continuation

Mafuyu hears the mother admit computer use and ask about poems, then infers that Nightcord has been found. The audience directly sees the search and knows its scope more precisely. She knows her attempted refusal at dinner failed and hears KAITO’s accusations, but rejects the claim about her mother’s intent. The father’s exact understanding of the concealed issue is not established (`PJSK:event:0089:02:003`; `PJSK:event:0089:05:003`; `PJSK:event:0089:06:002`).

Private listening, renewed lyric writing, insomnia and decisions about phone/light are audience-accessible but not shown transmitted to the group (`PJSK:card:0616:02`). Mafuyu explicitly reports unknown taste while accepting a small amount of parfait; Mizuki’s tentative appearance/smile readings remain inferences (`PJSK:card:0614:02:001:0052–0074`). A conditioned group visit establishes brief access and interest in the demo, not full resumption. She is not shown knowing the hotel meeting, maternal recruitment attempt or Kanade’s new refusal promise.

### `EPI-FAMILY-MAFUYU-MOTHER-E0089` — obtained records / friend identification / distorted reception

The mother directly finds concealed illustrations/poems, Nightcord, Yuki’s role and K’s composer message; the computer search/discovery uncertainty is resolved **from this event onward** (`PJSK:event:0089:01:005:0002–0027`). The meeting supplies Kanade’s real name and offline familiarity with Mafuyu (`PJSK:event:0089:07:002:0002–0020`). Kanade tells her the disappearance statement and present musical wish. She interprets the distress within a school/future frame and treats discussion as restoring the prescribed path (`PJSK:event:0089:08:002`).

Do not give her SEKAI knowledge, all friends’ faces, every group conversation, or Kanade’s private post-meeting thoughts. Her insistence that she knows Mafuyu’s best future is a professed position, not independent confirmation of that future or exhaustive access to her daughter’s experience.

### `EPI-N25-G-E0089` — limited transfers and deliberate withholding

Ena tells Kanade/Mizuki about the E0077 inquiry only after Mafuyu exits; Kanade tells them about contact/meeting but stops short of transmitting all dream/future-pressure details. They know the face-exposure plan and offer on-call help (`PJSK:event:0089:03:002:0093–0181`). Ena believes Mafuyu does not know the contact and may be better off not knowing, yet objects to major decisions being discussed without her (`PJSK:card:0613:01:001:0023–0025`; `PJSK:card:0613:02:001:0028–0032`). This tension is preserved.

The later introduction and blouse conversation do not certify full knowledge of the hotel exchange or Kanade’s private reasoning. Mafuyu’s own earlier disclosures and the others’ impressions must be tracked separately. The unnamed shop-working classmate in card 0614:02 remains unnamed; no roster-based identity transfer.

### `EPI-N25-VS-KAITO-E0089` — supplied history, tested assumptions, private revision

Miku supplies the host/group history, prior disappearance wishes and gradual positive changes. KAITO’s model of the mother and his reason for being called are interpretations of that information (`PJSK:card:0615:01`). The confrontation gives him direct evidence that Mafuyu has tried to say no; the later introduction adds names and Kanade’s objection to his total-no-resistance claim. His recollection leads to a private decision to consider another approach (`PJSK:card:0615:02:001:0017–0054`).

Luka explicitly saw the conversation without hearing its contents. KAITO’s thought that music is the sole thing reaching Mafuyu is his interpretation of Miku’s report, not a rejection of the envelope’s restaurant/refuge effects (`PJSK:card:0615:02:001:0060–0065`; `PJSK:area:areatalk_ev_night_12_004:01`). Agreed listening provides no reported critique. Rin/Len’s knowledge of Mafuyu’s avoidance grounds direct reassurance; no complete shared VS mind is assumed.

Next EVENT_0090. State and relationship successors operate with these knowledge limits; guarded-content nondisclosure, prior history and earlier bounded cross-unit ignorance remain intact.

**ADVANCE_RUI_SELF_KNOWLEDGE_WITH_EVENT_SCOPE.** I3 epistemic transition for Rui. AFTER: in core06:0075–0078 he tentatively recognizes fear that Tsukasa may leave and that he may lose his valued creative place. RETAINED USE: core07:0014–0017 acknowledges uncertainty and asks for participation; card0119:01:002:0019–0033 shows Rui distinguishing deliberate design/safety consideration from his earlier troubled restraint, with a private resolve not to lean on Tsukasa's receptiveness. Nene receives that stated distinction; the private resolve is Rui/reader knowledge. This is operative acquisition, not only a reader's revised interpretation of an unchanged motive. The attribution of an unconscious cause remains tentative; lasting freedom from distress, universally complete disclosure and general equipment safety are not established. All four human P06 IDs and company P06 remain unchanged. Other core acquisitions remain bounded: the four hear the grandfather/contest account, witness the accident and hear reported hospital results; Nene's private middle-school recollection and fear of renewed isolation are not automatically spoken; Emu separately hears Tsukasa's self-directed anger, without shown transfer of that full account to Rui/Nene/KAITO. Humans receive the interim result; core-ending KAITO merely hopes their outcome is good.

### MMJ-EP-HR-E0004 — EVENT_0004 bounded responsibility

**Prior and scope:** New bounded responsibility; no human successor implied. Causal home: [MMJ reading](../03_SEQUENTIAL_EVENT_READINGS/MMJ/PJSK_EVENT_0004_MMJ_INTEGRATION_CHECKPOINT.md).

Haruka learns a specific route into school belonging through preparation, received assistance, relay support, classmates' thanks, the accepted gathering and later ordinary contact.
